# Merged-roadmap work on the prerequisite chains of the 100 Annals definitions

Specified in a merged roadmap (`TauCetiRoadmap/<Name>/` at roadmap commit 592bdb1, 2026-11-23), not
yet (fully) implemented in Tau Ceti main (fb3fbc9f05, 2026-11-23), and on the prerequisite chain of at
least one definition. Each item was checked against the README section, STATUS.md (snapshot date
given per roadmap; several are stale) and a grep of Tau Ceti code. Item format: status
(NOT-STARTED / PARTIAL with what exists), ready now (yes / no: needs X), definitions served,
evidence. Section and layer ids are the roadmap's own.

## Summary: roadmaps ranked by number of the 100 definitions served

## Key ordering facts across items

- SchemeAndStackFoundations §4.14 (Hilbert/Quot) is the choke point for the Jacobian cluster
  (defs 19, 24, 30, 71, 80, 84, 85). Required order: JacobianChallenge §C.1 coherence (finiteness over a
  Noetherian ring is in code for projective schemes and the relative projective case is ready; the proper case then follows by Chow's lemma over an affine base, which
  is in code) and §C.2 base change, then SSF §4.14, then §D.3 Hilbert quotients and §D.4 projective
  Picard representability, then §D.5 `Pic⁰` and the Abel maps and §D.8 the Jacobian. §D.2
  (`HasPicardScheme`, `picardScheme`) is in code. `Symᵈ X` (§C.4) needs only §C.2, not §4.14; §D.5
  needs it.
- SSF Layer 1 is a linear chain: §1.3 (done), then §1.5, §1.6, §1.7/§1.8, §1.9, then §1.19, §1.20,
  §1.21, §1.22, §1.23. §1.5 is partial (`EtaleEquivRel` with `restrict` in code; the quotient sheaf
  and `EtaleEquivRel.ofAtlas` are in separate open PRs), so §1.6 waits on §1.5's quotient sheaf. §1.18 has `StackInGroupoids`,
  `ofSheaf`/`ofScheme`, `yonedaEquiv`, the generic 2-fibre product and a stackification pseudofunctor in code; the
  stack property and universality of stackification are ready now. §1.19's `IsRepresentableBySpaces`
  is in an open PR.
- LieGroups Layer 7 (compact real form, Cartan involution θ) unblocks Layer 9 beyond `GL n`. Layer 9
  in turn heads the real-reductive chain: ArithmeticLocallySymmetricSpaces ALS.F.1 and ALS.0.1–ALS.0.3,
  then AutomorphicFormsOnReductiveGroups §2.3a and §§2.3–2.4, then RealSemisimpleLieGroups Layers 2–5
  and §5.7, for defs 1, 4, 7, 15, 31, 54, 62, 79. Layer 7 is partial (`serreCompactInvolution`, the
  conjugate-linear Chevalley involution of a Serre presentation) and ready now. The Cartan-involution
  predicates are in code at the Lie-algebra level (`LieEquiv.IsCartanInvolution`, with the Cartan
  decomposition) and for real affine groups (`TauCeti.IsCartanInvolution`, ALS.0.1); existence and
  conjugacy of Cartan involutions are not.
- EtaleCohomologyFoundations §F.0.1 (étale module sheaves) precedes every étale cohomology item
  (§F.1, §F.2.x, §F.4.x). It is partial (geometric stalks, finite lisse sheaves and the monoidal
  structure with monoidal stalks in code), and its constructible part is ready now.
- RiemannianGeometry R5a (B, S, normal connection) is formally blocked on SmoothBundlesConnections
  B3–B4 per its README, though that dependency looks soft: the normal bundle `ν_f` with its
  projection and the derivative along a map `CovariantDerivative.alongMap` are in code. It heads the
  chain R5a, then R5a.2 (with R5a.1, which is partial and ready now), then R5b (whose operator part,
  `IsCoercive.formOperator`, is in code), then R5c, then MinimalSurfaces MS6, for defs 49, 52, 65,
  66. Varifolds §5.1 and RiemannianManifoldsWithConvexBoundary §3.6 also consume R5a.
- The boundary tangent space and the outward unit normal (RiemannianManifoldsWithConvexBoundary §1.1,
  `outwardUnitNormal`) are in code, so RiemannianManifoldsWithConvexBoundary §§1.2–1.3 and
  RiemannianGeometry R7.9 are ready (defs 49, 52, 66). DifferentialGeometry 12.4 still waits on
  `integralDensity` (open PR #38427).
- SchemeAndStackFoundations §§1.19–1.20 (`IsAlgebraicStack`, points of a stack) head all of
  StackPointsAndResidualGerbes (defs 16, 17, 28, 29), none of whose items can start before them;
  they sit at the end of the SSF Layer 1 chain above.
- VirtualFundamentalClass (defs 16, 27) starts at Layer 0 (stack dimension), which needs SSF Layer 1
  through §1.19 (DM stacks) and §1.21 (quotient stacks); its Layer 1 also needs SSF §§1.10, 1.22 and
  §5.5 (partial: the normal cone of an immersion is in code), and its Layer 3 the cotangent-complex
  chain DerivedModuleAlgebra §0.4, then DerivedDeRhamCohomology §5.8. No item can start now.
- AlgebraicModuliForArithmeticGeometry §1.3 `coherentGrassmannian` (needing ModularCurves §0.7 over
  a non-affine base, in an open PR) blocks NashBlowups §0.4 and §§1.2–1.5, hence Layers 2, 6 and 7
  (defs 25, 26). NashBlowups §§0.1–0.2 and §0.7 are partial; §0.2's closed-immersion clause, §0.3
  and the rest of §0.6 can start now, and the rest of §0.7 follows §0.3.
- LieGroups Layer 9 (`CartanInvolution`, `IwasawaData` beyond `GL n`) also blocks LieGroupsPartII
  Layers 1–3 and §§7.1, 8.1 (defs 1, 4, 54, 79).
- The modular-curve cluster (defs 69, 71, 77) runs through ModularCurvesPartII, whose items wait on
  ModularCurves Layer 10 (untouched), JacobianChallengePartII Layers 0–1 (on JacobianChallenge
  Layers D–E) and EtaleCohomologyFoundations §§F.1–F.4. Only ModularCurvesPartII §3.8 (partial) can
  start now. CompletedCohomologyPartII and CompletedCohomologyAndLocalGlobalCompatibility §§1.1–1.2
  (def 77) wait on the same ModularCurvesPartII §§14.1–14.4a and on ArithmeticLocallySymmetricSpaces
  §§0.1–0.7.
- ShimuraVarieties §§P.1–P.5 (Hodge structures as representations of the Deligne torus) head both
  Mumford–Tate specifications, ShimuraVarieties §P.3a and ShimuraData Layer 1 (defs 13, 23, 89).
  The direction from Hodge structures to Deligne-torus actions on points, the Hodge decomposition of
  a real `S`-comodule (`hodgeComponent`) and the Mumford–Tate Hopf ideal of an algebraic `h`
  (`mumfordTateHopfIdeal`) are in code; the passage from a Hodge structure
  to an algebraic `h` (§P.3) is ready and is the step that makes the construction apply to #13.
- BallQuotientsAndComplexHyperbolicLattices (defs 15, 31) starts from RealSemisimpleLieGroups §5.7,
  hence from LieGroups Layer 9, and its quotients need ComplexComparisonPartII §C-1.1.
- ModularCharactersAndBlocks §0.2 (block idempotents) is partial and §6.1 (relative traces) can
  start now; with §1.3 (ready, open PR) they head the chain to blocks, defect groups and heights
  (defs 2, 3, 9), and ModularCharactersAndBlocksPartII Layer 6 builds heights on them. Its Layers 1–5
  (Glauberman correspondence, def 8) need only Mathlib and Tau Ceti character theory at the start.
- The CM chain (defs 70, 74, 75) starts from ShimuraVarieties §5.1 `CMType` (ready, open PRs), then
  ComplexMultiplicationAndExplicitReciprocity §1.2 (whose algebraic splitting part can start now),
  then AutomorphicPadicLFunctionsPartII §§0.1, 0.2 and 5.1; the abelian-scheme parts also need
  AbelianSchemesAndArithmeticModuli Layers A1–A5.
- QuasiregularMaps (defs 41, 42) starts with §1.1 `minimalStretch` (ready, open PR); its §§2.1–2.3
  also need SobolevMapsBetweenManifolds §§4.2–4.3, §5.1 and §§6.2–6.3 (def 47).
- The Néron–Tate height (def 80) follows HeightsRationalPointsAndObstructions §§0.1–0.8 and §0.18
  (ready, open PRs), then §0.19, which needs AbelianSchemesAndArithmeticModuli A1.4–A1.5.
- ReductiveGroups §§9B.5–9B.8 (def 11) define the classical group schemes, which can start now on
  `TauCeti.ConstantForm` and the Clifford API; their comparisons with pinned models wait on
  Layer 9's uniform pinned construction and §§9B.1–9B.3, and the Dickson invariant and smoothness
  on LefschetzPencilsAndVanishingCycles §§2.1–2.2 and DeformationAndDerivedPatchingAlgebra §3.3.

### §1.4 AlgSpace.overEquiv (algebraic spaces over a base)
- Status: PARTIAL: §1.3 is complete in code: the predicates `SchemePresheaf`, `Spaces.RepresentableDiagonal`, `Spaces.EtaleAtlas`, `Spaces.IsAlgebraicSpace` (TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry/AlgebraicSpace/Basic.lean), `Spaces.isAlgebraicSpace_of_etale_cover` with `EtaleAtlas.of_sigma` (AlgebraicSpace/EtaleCover.lean) and `IsAlgebraicSpace.diagonal_locallyQuasiFinite` with `EtaleAtlas.locallyOfFiniteType_diag` (AlgebraicSpace/Diagonal.lean). §1.4 has `Spaces.AlgSpace`, `toPresheaf`, `ofScheme` (Full/Faithful), `homFromSchemeEquiv`, terminal `Spec ℤ`, initial `∅` (AlgebraicSpace/Category.lean). The presheaf-level input exists: `TauCeti.overYonedaEquivPresheafOver X : Over (yoneda.obj X) ≌ (Over X)ᵒᵖ ⥤ Type` with `overYonedaEquivPresheafOverYonedaIso` (TauCeti/CategoryTheory/Comma/Presheaf/OverYoneda.lean). Missing: `IsAlgebraicSpaceOver S` and `AlgSpace.overEquiv` (algebraic spaces over `h_S` ≃ fppf sheaves on `Over S` satisfying `IsAlgebraicSpaceOver S`). Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/43599 (AlgebraicSpace/Over.lean: `IsAlgebraicSpaceOver S` and the equivalence `AlgSpace.overEquiv S`, completing the §1.4 declarations). Open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/3823 (the §1.4 Checks: `Hom(h_∅, F)` and `Hom(F, h_{Spec ℤ})` are points, `Hom(h_{Spec k}, h_∅)` is empty, and the endomorphisms of `h_{Spec K}` over `h_{Spec ℚ}` for a quadratic `K/ℚ`, Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/AlgebraicSpace/Category.lean).
- Ready now: yes (needs only §1.3, which is done; the presheaf equivalence is in code)
- Serves defs: 16, 20, 28
- Evidence: README l.2899 "### 1.4 The category of algebraic spaces" (l.2905 `IsAlgebraicSpaceOver S`); STATUS Layer 1 remaining "... algebraic spaces over a base (1.4), quotient sheaf of 1.5 onward"; grep `overEquiv|IsAlgebraicSpaceOver` in TauCeti/ empty.

### §1.5 étale equivalence relations and the quotient sheaf
- Status: PARTIAL: `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.Spaces.EtaleEquivRel s t` (Mathlib's internal `IsEquivalenceRelation t s` with `s`, `t` étale) with `refl`, `symm`, `trans` and their laws, `swap`, `hom_ext`, `etale_refl`, `etale_trans`, `id`, `of_isKernelPair` and `EtaleEquivRel.restrict` (the restriction `R ×_{U × U} (U' × U')` along an étale `U' ⟶ U`) (TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry/AlgebraicSpace/EtaleEquivRel.lean), on `TauCeti.CategoryTheory.isEquivalenceRelation_iff` (jointly monic plus an equivalence relation on `T`-points, the README's `IsEquivRel`), `IsKernelPair.isEquivalenceRelation` and `IsEquivalenceRelation.id` (TauCeti/CategoryTheory/EquivalenceRelation.lean). STATUS.md snapshot: 38bee4a 2026-11-19, generated against Tau Ceti bf5b062 (Tau Ceti checked at fb3fbc9f05; README at roadmap 886c89ef3)

### Layer 5 (solvable groups) Structure of smooth connected solvable groups, G = G_u ⋊ T
- Status: PARTIAL. Exists: `torus_coinvariants_unipotentRadical` (G/G_u is a torus), and the triviality of homomorphisms from diagonalizable groups to unipotent groups, scheme-theoretically, so a diagonalizable closed subgroup of a unipotent group is trivial (`TauCeti.geometricallyUnipotentPointsCommHopfAlgProperty.bialgHom_eq_unit_comp_counit_of_groupLikeSetSpan_eq_top`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Unipotent/Diagonalizable.lean, on `Module.Basis.map_coefficientMatrix_eq_one_of_isUpperTriangular_of_groupLikeSetSpan_eq_top`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Representation/UpperUnitriangular.lean); separable base change and descent of the unipotent radical. Missing: the consequence that a maximal torus T meets G_u trivially and maps isomorphically onto G/G_u, the decomposition G = G_u ⋊ T, and conjugacy of subgroups of multiplicative type into T by G_u(k̄) (Milne, Theorem 16.33(b),(d)). STATUS lists Layer 5 as done. Open Tau Ceti PRs https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/43516 (a diagonalizable closed subgroup or torus meets `R_u(G)` trivially and maps with trivial kernel to `G ⧸ R_u(G)`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Unipotent/Radical/Diagonalizable/) and https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/44028 (split extensions of affine groups are semidirect products: if a closed subgroup `T` maps isomorphically onto `G ⧸ R_u(G)` then `R_u(G) ⋊ T → G` is an isomorphism, HopfIdeal/Normal/Product/Split.lean); together they reduce `G = G_u ⋊ T` to the isomorphism of a maximal torus onto `G ⧸ G_u`, which neither proves. Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45234 (a homomorphism of finite-type affine groups with smooth source and trivial kernel is a closed immersion (Milne 5.34), and a closed immersion of smooth connected groups not dropping the Lie dimension is an isomorphism, applied to a torus `T ≤ G` and `G ⧸ R_u(G)`, `surjective_iff_kernelHopfIdeal_eq_augmentation_of_isGeometricallyReduced`, `isIso_of_kernelHopfIdeal_eq_augmentation_of_finrank_le`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/HopfIdeal/Quotient/Kernel/ClosedImmersion.lean, Unipotent/Radical/Torus.lean; with #43516 and #44028 it leaves the dimension count `dim T = dim G/G_u`). - Status: PARTIAL, all but the converse criterion: perimeter API, essential and reduced boundaries, outer normal, density estimates, blow-ups (Analysis/BoundedVariation/BlowUp.lean), countable `(n−1)`-rectifiability of the reduced boundary `countablyRectifiable_reducedBoundary` (Analysis/BoundedVariation/Rectifiable.lean), De Giorgi's theorem `|Dχ_E| = Hⁿ⁻¹⌞∂*E` (`variationMeasure_eq_comap_restrict_reducedBoundary`, `perimeter_eq_euclideanHausdorffMeasure_reducedBoundary`) with Gauss–Green against `Hⁿ⁻¹` on the reduced boundary and `P(E; Ω) ≤ Hⁿ⁻¹(∂E ∩ Ω)` for sets of locally finite perimeter (`perimeter_le_euclideanHausdorffMeasure_frontier_inter`) (Analysis/BoundedVariation/DeGiorgi.lean), and the essential/reduced comparison up to an `Hⁿ⁻¹`-null set `essentialBoundary_inter_ae_eq_reducedBoundary` (Analysis/BoundedVariation/EssentialBoundary.lean). - Ready now: yes (all of G5's other targets are in code)

Bundle sections: the Banach space `TauCeti.HolderSection A k α` of sections of a vector bundle whose chart readings over a `FiniteBundleAtlas` are `C^{k,α}` (`FiniteBundleAtlas.holderSections`, `isClosed_holderSections`, `MemHolderSection`, Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/Holder/{Basic,ContMDiffSection}.lean). Euclidean inputs: `TauCeti.CkHolderSpaceOn` (Analysis/Holder/OpenSet.lean), the identification of the non-integer-order Hölder–Zygmund space with the bounded Hölder space `TauCeti.BesovSpace.ckHolderSpaceEquiv` (Analysis/Holder/Zygmund.lean), and the Euclidean mapping `B^s_{p,q} → B^{s−m}_{p,q}` of uniform symbols, including the Hölder–Zygmund case `p = q = ∞` (`TauCeti.Symbol.quantizeBesov`, `TemperedDistribution.MemBesov.quantizeTempered`). STATUS.md snapshot: bf232a2 2026-11-16, generated against Tau Ceti aa6e43e (Tau Ceti checked at fb3fbc9f05, 2026-11-23)

### Layer 8 Hochschild cochains (ordinary algebras, normalized, HH²)
- Status: PARTIAL. Positive-arity Hochschild cochains of an A∞ algebra, with the Hochschild differential `[m,-]`, braces, the Gerstenhaber bracket and cohomology (Algebra/Homology/AInfinity/Algebra/Hochschild/{Basic,Complex,Cohomology}.lean). Normalized cochains for a strict unit, `PositiveHochschild.normalizedComplex` (Hochschild/Normalized.lean); whether the inclusion is a quasi-isomorphism is not addressed. Formal deformations and obstruction classes in Hochschild degree two (Deformation.lean). An ordinary or DG algebra becomes an A∞ algebra via `IsNonUnitalDGAlgebra.toAInfinityAlgebra` (DG.lean). For an ordinary algebra `A` and an `A`-bimodule `M`, the Hochschild coboundary `TauCeti.hochschildBimoduleCoboundary` (RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/Coboundary.lean) and the cochain complex and cohomology with bimodule coefficients, `TauCeti.hochschildBimoduleCochainComplex`, `hochschildBimoduleCohomology` (RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/Bimodule.lean, Tau Ceti PR #37750); matrix Morita invariance of `HHⁿ`, `TauCeti.hochschildCohomology.matrixExtensionEquiv` (HochschildCohomology/Matrix.lean). Missing: HH² ↔ infinitesimal deformations and HH³ ↔ obstructions for ordinary algebras, normalization comparison, and quasi-equivalence/Morita invariance beyond matrix algebras. Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45282 (HH² classifies infinitesimal deformations of an ordinary algebra: `TauCeti.IsInfinitesimalDeformation` with `isInfinitesimalDeformation_iff_mem_hochschildBimoduleCocycles` and `TauCeti.InfinitesimalDeformation.equivHochschildBimoduleCohomology`, RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/InfinitesimalDeformation.lean) and https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45290 (HH³ obstructions: `TauCeti.IsAlgebraDeformationOfOrder`, the obstruction `TauCeti.algebraDeformationObstruction` is a Hochschild 3-cocycle, and its class `IsAlgebraDeformationOfOrder.obstructionClass` in `HH³(A, A)` vanishes exactly when the deformation extends, RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/DeformationObstruction.lean); together they cover the ordinary-algebra clause, and the normalization comparison remains.
- Ready now: yes. The README names no blocking prerequisites for the ordinary-algebra HH²/HH³ clause, and both the A∞ machinery it specializes and the bimodule-coefficient cohomology are present.

- Status: PARTIAL: the structure, its equivalences and pullback are in code, on a general carrier for lifts of the orthonormal frame bundle along a homomorphism `ρ : G →* (Fin n → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin n → ℝ)`: `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameLift` with `OrthonormalFrameLift.Equiv` (`refl`, `symm`, `trans`), `pullback`, `Equiv.pullback` and extension of lifts along structure-group homomorphisms (Topology/VectorBundle/OrthonormalFrame/Lift.lean); `TauCeti.SpinStructure` for rank `n ≥ 1` (a lift for `realCliffordSpinGroupZero n` along `CliffordAlgebra.spinVectorAction`, no numerability field, as the README's `Suggested.lean` specifies), with the sheet exchange `OrthonormalFrameLift.Equiv.negOne`, the induced orientation `SpinStructure.orientation` and the lifted frame map into oriented frames `SpinStructure.toOrientedFrames` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/{Basic,Orientation}.lean); `TauCeti.SpinCStructure` on the same carrier. Missing: descent, metric-change independence, smooth structures on lifts, and G4.3 onward. G4.1a–b frame adapters are done. The carriers the section names exist: `TauCeti.PrincipalBundle` with `PrincipalBundle.pullback`, cocycle gluing `TauCeti.fiberBundleCoreOfGroupCocycle`, `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameBundle` with its presentation as a principal `O(n)`-bundle, and `CliffordAlgebra.realCliffordSpinGroupZero`. Open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/2658 (transport Spin structures and frame-bundle lifts along bundle isometries, G4.2).
- Ready now: no for the rest: descent and metric-change independence need TopologicalKTheory T6.4's numerable principal-bundle category and CharacteristicClasses C1.0's oriented frames and metric-change transport (neither in code). FourManifolds §4.1, the target for def 64, builds its Spin^c lifts on G4's frame lifts and covering adapters (FourManifolds README l.1695).
- Serves defs: 64
- Evidence: README l.1044 "## Layer 4: Spin lifts, obstruction and classification", l.1168 "### 4.2 Principal spin lifts (G4.2)" ("A spin structure on an oriented metric V of rank n is a numerable principal Spin(n)-bundle P with an equivariant map P→Fr⁺_SO(V)"), *Needs* l.1235; STATUS Layer 4 remaining "descent, metric change and smooth lifts (G4.2); the spin obstruction, torsor of lifts and rank-two criterion (G4.3-G4.4a)".

### §4.1 (group and lift part) Spinᶜ(n), its maps to SO(n) and U(1), Spinᶜ structures as principal lifts with their isomorphisms and pullback
- Status: PARTIAL: the group is in code: `CliffordAlgebra.spinCGroup Q hQ` = (Spin(Q) × `Circle`)/`spinCKernel` with `mk`, `lift`, `ofSpin`, `ofCircle`, the determinant character `det` (`det_mk : det [s, z] = z²`), `toSpecialOrthogonal`, `mem_ker_toSpecialOrthogonal_prod_det_iff`, `finite_ker_toSpecialOrthogonal_prod_det` and charge conjugation `chargeConj` (LinearAlgebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC.lean); continuity, `detContinuousMonoidHom`, `ofCircleContinuousMonoidHom`, `chargeConjContinuousMulEquiv`, `isQuotientCoveringMap_toSpecialOrthogonal_prod_det` (Topology/Algebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC.lean); the smooth compact Lie-group structure, `contMDiff_realDet`, `contMDiff_realChargeConj` (Geometry/Lie/CliffordAlgebra/SpinC/Basic.lean), `realDetLieLift` (SpinC/Determinant.lean) and half-spin actions (SpinC/HalfSpin.lean); the canonical lift `TauCeti.SeibergWitten.unitaryLift : U(V, J) → Spinᶜ(4)` (Analysis/PDE/SeibergWitten/UnitaryLift.lean). The lift is in code: `TauCeti.SpinCStructure`, a principal `Spinᶜ(n)`-bundle with a frame map to the orthonormal frame bundle, as an `OrthonormalFrameLift` along `spinCGroup.toSpecialOrthogonal`, with `toFrame_smul_mk`, `toFrame_smul_ofCircle` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/Basic.lean), its isomorphisms `OrthonormalFrameLift.Equiv` and pullbacks `OrthonormalFrameLift.pullback`, `Equiv.pullback` (Topology/VectorBundle/OrthonormalFrame/Lift.lean), and structures induced along a lift of the structure group, `OrthonormalFrameLift.extendSpinC` (which gives the Spin-induced and the unitary `Spinᶜ` structures). The determinant line is in code on `TauCeti.AssociatedBundle`: `TauCeti.SpinCStructure.determinantLine` (`P ×_{z²} ℂ`, with `finrank_determinantLine` and the weight-two central action `mk_ofCircle_smul_determinantLine`), with the rank-four half-spinor bundles `SpinCStructure.halfSpinorBundle`, Clifford multiplication `SpinCStructure.cliffordMul` and `exteriorPowerHalfSpinorEquiv` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/Spinor.lean, for SeibergWittenTheory SW1.2). Missing: twisting by a line, sums, orientation change, boundary restriction and compatibility with Spin structures (the `Spinᶜ` structure of a `SpinStructure` with trivial determinant line). Open Tau Ceti PRs https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45347 (twisting by a principal `U(1)`-bundle, on the fibre product `TauCeti.PrincipalBundle.FiberProd` and `OrthonormalFrameLift.fiberProd`; the determinant-line comparison `c₁ ↦ c₁ + 2a` remains) and https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45352 (orientation reversal `TauCeti.SpinCStructure.reverse`, through `SpinCStructure.precompIsometry` along an isometry of the model and `OrthonormalFrameLift.precomp`) and https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/3693 (the structure-group part of boundary restriction: `Spinᶜ(n)` for the standard form `CliffordAlgebra.realCliffordSpinCGroupZero` and the inclusion `CliffordAlgebra.realCliffordSpinCInclusion : Spinᶜ(n) → Spinᶜ(n + 1)` onto the stabilizer of the last coordinate vector, `range_realCliffordSpinCInclusion`, a closed embedding of topological groups). Open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/3729 (compatibility with Spin structures: `SpinStructure.toSpinC` with the nowhere-vanishing section `determinantLineSection` of its determinant line, Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/OfSpin.lean). Open Tau Ceti PRs https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/3768 (locally trivial reductions of the frame bundle as `OrthonormalFrameLift`s, `OrthonormalFrameBundle.reductionLift`, and the unitary frame lift `unitaryFrameLift` along `U(2) ⊆ SO(4)`, OrthonormalFrame/ReductionLift.lean) and https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/3770 (frames adapted to a unit section, `OrthonormalFrameBundle.sectionFrames`, locally trivial with fibre `O(N − 1)`, OrthonormalFrame/UnitSection.lean: the bundle step of boundary restriction). Open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/3854 (*sums*, group part: `CliffordAlgebra.spinCGroupProd : Spinᶜ(Q₁) × Spinᶜ(Q₂) →* Spinᶜ(Q₁ ⊕ Q₂)` with its kernel, determinant and rotation, LinearAlgebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC/Prod.lean). The bundle-level sum and the restriction of a lift to a boundary remain.
- Ready now: yes: the *Needs* item it uses is SpinGeometry G4's frame lifts and covering adapters, in code as `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameLift` and `TauCeti.SpinStructure`.
